root@01867ae1ea67:/app# python3 -c "
> from PIL import Image
> img = Image.open('chess_puzzle.png')
> img = img.convert('L')
> width, height = img.size
> square_size = min(width, height) // 8
> print('Square positions:')
> for rank in range(1, 9):
>     for file in range(1, 9):
>         x0 = (file - 1) * square_size
>         x1 = x0 + square_size
>         y0 = (8 - rank) * square_size
>         y1 = y0 + square_size
>         square = img.crop((x0, y0, x1, y1))
>         pixels = square.getdata()
>         avg = sum(pixels) / len(pixels)
>         if avg > 200:
>             typ = 'W'
>         elif avg < 50:
>             typ = 'B'
>         else:
>             typ = '.'
>         print(f'{rank}{chr(ord(\"a\") + file - 1)}: {typ} avg={avg:.2f}')
> "
